Cobot Controller Communication Instruction
Protocol (V3.9.8)

I

lllustrate

Illustrate:
(1) This manual is applicable to cobots of WebApp version 3.9.8, and the
contents of the manual are subject to change without notice. To view other
versions of the cobot controller communication command protocol, please log in
to the FAO documentation:
Chinese
(simplified
Chinese)
https://fairino-doc-zhs.readthedocs.io/

online

document

link

：

Chinese (Traditional) link： https://fairino-doc-zht.readthedocs.io/
English link： https://fairino-doc-en.readthedocs.io/
Japanese link： https://fairino-doc-ja.readthedocs.io/
(2) All protocol instruction examples in this manual are based on the FR5
robot as an example and are for reference only. Different robot models and
different postures will lead to different parameters, please refer to the specific
protocol format for the actual operation protocol.

instruction.

– Read rotation clamping claw status;
